completion else \\\"\\\"\\n    return completion\\n\\n\\ndef make_reward_fn(reward_mode: str, solver, exec_weight: float = 0.5, sta_weight: float = 0.5, logic_type: str = \\\"fol\\\", beta: float = 0.3, beta_schedule: str = \\\"fixed\\\", beta_schedule_midpoint: int = 250, max_steps: int = 500):\\n    stats = {\\\"total\\\": 0, \\\"parsed\\\": 0, \\\"proved\\\": 0, \\\"isr_sum\\\": 0.0}\\n    step_buf = {\\\"total\\\": 0, \\\"parsed\\\": 0, \\\"proved\\\": 0, \\\"isr_sum\\\": 0.0, \\\"reward_sum\\\": 0.0, \\\"random_sum\\\": 0.0, \\\"random_n\\\": 0, \\\"beta_t\\\": None}\\n    schedule_state = {\\\"current_step\\\": 0}\\n\\n    def compute_beta_t():\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute time-varying beta coefficient based on the chosen schedule.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        step = schedule_state[\\\"current_step\\\"]\\n        if beta_schedule == \\\"linear\\\":\\n            # beta_t = beta * (t / T): linear ramp from 0 to beta over max_steps\\n            return beta * min(step / max_steps, 1.0) if max_steps > 0 else beta\\n        elif beta_schedule == \\\"sigmoid\\\":\\n            # beta_t = beta * sigma((t - midpoint) / (T/10)): S-curve centered at midpoint\\n            x = (step - beta_schedule_midpoint) / (max_steps / 10) if max_steps > 0 else 0\\n            return beta / (1 + math.exp(-x))\\n        else:\\n            return beta\\n\\n\\n    def reward_fn(prompts, completions, **kwargs):\\n        rewards = []\\n        for completion in completions:\\n            text = extract_completion_text(completion)\\n            cleaned = strip_thinking(text)\\n            stats[\\\"total\\\"] += 1\\n            step_buf[\\\"total\\\"] += 1\\n\\n            if logic_type == \\\"prolog\\\":\\n                _fast_fn = fast_prolog_sta_reward\\n                _full_fn = prolog_sta_reward\\n            else:\\n                _fast_fn = fast_sta_reward\\n                _full_fn = sta_reward\\n\\n            if reward_mode == \\\"execution_only\\\":\\n                result = _full_fn(\\n                    {}, cleaned, solver,\\n                    execution_weight=1.0, sta_weight=0.0, timeout=5,\\n                )\\n                reward_val = result[\\\"execution_reward\\\"]\\n            elif reward_mode == \\\"random_reward\\\":\\n                result = _full_fn(\\n                    {}, cleaned, solver,\\n                    execution_weight=1.0, sta_weight=0.0, timeout=5,\\n                )\\n                proved = result[\\\"execution_reward\\\"] > 0\\n                if proved:\\n                    rng = _random.Random(stats[\\\"total\\\"])\\n                    rand_val = rng.random()\\n                    reward_val = exec_weight * 1.0 + sta_weight * rand_val\\n                    result[\\\"random_reward\\\"] = rand_val\\n                    step_buf[\\\"random_sum\\\"] += rand_val\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": Now I need to verify the constraints: `save_total_limit=1` is already set in GRPOConfig, and I should confirm `log_completions=False` is configured. The GPU check shows cuda:1 is clear at 0MiB. Setting up the tmux session...

I need to verify the log directory exists and check available disk space before running this. Looking at the reward formulations more carefully, I see that plan004 uses `r = exec_weight * exec_reward + sta_weight * fast_sta_reward` with equal weights of 0.5, which differs from plan002's additive bonus approach where `r = exec * (1 + 0.3 * full_ISR)`. The key difference is that the O(1) binary ISR returns 1.0 for all proved samples, potentially losing the partial redundancy signal that the full STA captures.
